# Zova SaaS - Comprehensive Design System & Architecture Document

Welcome to the official, highly detailed Design System and UI Architecture documentation for the Zova SaaS platform. This document defines the visual language, motion guidelines, component specifications, typography scales, color palettes, and overall aesthetic philosophy driving the Zova SaaS user interface.

## Table of Contents
1. Design Philosophy
2. Core Design Tokens
    - Color System
    - Typography System
    - Spacing System
    - Shadows & Elevation
    - Radii & Borders
3. Layout & Grid Specifications
4. Animation & Motion Design (Framer Motion)
5. Doodles & Illustrations
6. Component Library Specifications
7. The Halo Effect Background
8. Accessibility Guidelines
9. Implementation Details (Tailwind + React)
10. Expanded Component Code Implementations

---

## 1. Design Philosophy

The Zova SaaS website embodies a Modern Minimalist aesthetic with strong Glassmorphism influences. It aims to provide a pristine, professional, yet approachable vibe. Principle 1: Focus on absolute clarity and visual hierarchy through whitespace and typography.

### Shadows & Elevation

Shadows are incredibly soft, utilizing low opacity and high blur to create a premium glassmorphism elevation.

#### Elevation 1
```css
.shadow-elevation-1 {
  box-shadow: 0px 2px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05);
}
```

```css
  box-shadow: 0px 4px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05);
}
```

## 3. Layout & Grid Specifications

### 1 Column Grid
```css
.grid-cols-1 { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(1, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 1.5rem; }
```

### 2 Column Grid
```css
.grid-cols-2 { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 1.5rem; }
```

## 4. Animation & Motion Design (Framer Motion)

The animations are built on Framer Motion using physics-based spring configurations.

### Spring Config Variant 1
```javascript
export const springConfig1 = {
  type: 'spring',
  stiffness: 205,
  damping: 21,
  mass: 1.1
};
```

## 6. Component Library Specifications

### Button - Default Variant
```jsx
import React from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';

export const ButtonDefault = ({ children }) => (
  <motion.div
    whileHover={ scale: 1.05 }
    whileTap={ scale: 0.95 }
    className='p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm bg-white border border-gray-100'
  >
    {children}
  </motion.div>
);
```

```jsx
    whileHover={ scale: 1.05 }
    whileTap={ scale: 0.95 }
    className='p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm bg-white border border-gray-100'
  >
    {children}
  </motion.div>
);
```

## 7. The Halo Effect Background

### Halo Layer 1
```css
.halo-layer-1 {
  position: absolute;
  top: 5%;
  left: 49%;
  width: 10vw;
  height: 10vh;
  background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(51, 136, 255, 0.2) 0%, transparent 70%);
  filter: blur(10px);
  z-index: -1;
}
```

## 10. Expanded Component Code Implementations

### Complex Page Section 1
```jsx
export default function ComplexSection1() {
  return (
    <section className='py-24 px-6 md:px-12 max-w-7xl mx-auto'>
      <div className='flex flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-8'>
        <h2 className='text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold tracking-tight text-center'>Section 1 Heading</h2>
        <p className='text-xl text-gray-600 max-w-2xl text-center'>Detailed description for section 1 emphasizing the premium aesthetic.</p>
        <div className='grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-8 w-full mt-12'>
          {[1, 2, 3].map(item => (
            <div key={item} className='bg-white p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-elevation-5 border border-gray-100'>
              <div className='w-12 h-12 bg-blue-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-6 text-blue-600'>
                <svg className='w-6 h-6' fill='none' stroke='currentColor' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path strokeLinecap='round' strokeLinejoin='round' strokeWidth='2' d='M13 10V3L4 14h7v7l9-11h-7z'></path></svg>
              </div>
              <h3 className='text-xl font-semibold mb-3'>Feature {item}</h3>
              <p className='text-gray-500'>Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet, consectetur adipiscing elit.</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```
